Append a Clean End Card
was 230


Add a logo or text overlay · Tests appending an accurately timed end card with exact text while preserving the complete source clip and its audio.
User Keep this whole 7-second clip, then add a 2-second black end card with white text: 'Ready for takeoff?' and '@flightschool' underneath. Center both lines. Keep the original sound, but make the end card silent. + radio-lines.mp4
Agent The connected Magic Hour tools don’t support exact clip concatenation. Using its generative editor could change the original footage or sound, so I haven’t run it.

This **FFmpeg** command preserves the full 7-second clip and its audio, then adds a silent 2-second black card with both white lines centered:

```bash
ffmpeg -i radio-lines.mp4 -filter_complex \
"[0:v]setpts=PTS-STARTPTS,tpad=stop_mode=add:stop_duration=2:color=black,\
drawtext=text='Ready for takeoff?':fontcolor=white:fontsize=48:x=(w-tw)/2:y=h/2-54:enable='gte(t,7)',\
drawtext=text='@flightschool':fontcolor=white:fontsize=36:x=(w-tw)/2:y=h/2+18:enable='gte(t,7)'[v];\
[0:a]asetpts=PTS-STARTPTS,apad=pad_dur=2[a]" \
-map "[v]" -map "[a]" -t 9 -c:v libx264 -crf 18 -c:a aac \
-movflags +faststart radio-lines-final.mp4
```

This requires FFmpeg with the `drawtext` filter installed.
